The second type of broadcast is based on the <math id="A2.SSx1.p4.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}_{\max}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}_{\max}</annotation></semantics></math> values: if
the <math id="A2.SSx1.p4.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}_{\max}(u)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}_{\max}(u)</annotation></semantics></math> drops by a <math id="A2.SSx1.p4.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor, we reassign all
edges assigned to <math id="A2.SSx1.p4.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math>.</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx1.p5" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx1.p5.1" class="ltx_p">We first state a useful invariant about the approximate weights, <math id="A2.SSx1.p5.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}</annotation></semantics></math>.
Before any merges are performed, the weights are exact.
There may be three things affecting <math id="A2.SSx1.p5.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="w(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>w</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">w(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math>: the cut-size changing, or the
cluster size of either <math id="A2.SSx1.p5.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math> or <math id="A2.SSx1.p5.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math> changing. Changes of the first type are easy to
handle, and can update the weight of the edge to be exact. Changing the cluster size
of the endpoint to which the edge is assigned to (say <math id="A2.SSx1.p5.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math>) is also easy, since the
weight is implicitly normalized by <math id="A2.SSx1.p5.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="|u|" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">|</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">|</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">|u|</annotation></semantics></math>. So the only error comes from changes to the
neighbor cluster size, <math id="A2.SSx1.p5.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math>, but since we broadcast and make the incident edge weights exact
after a cluster grows in size by a <math id="A2.SSx1.p5.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor, the edge weight can be at most a <math id="A2.SSx1.p5.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor larger than the true weight.
This guarantee for the approximate weights, <math id="A2.SSx1.p5.m10" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}</annotation></semantics></math>, provided by the broadcasting procedure is summarized by the next invariant:</p>
</div>
<div id="Thminvariant1" class="ltx_theorem ltx_theorem_invariant">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_smallcaps ltx_title_theorem">Invariant 1.</h6>
<div id="Thminvariant1.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="Thminvariant1.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="Thminvariant1.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">For every edge <math id="Thminvariant1.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,v)\in E,w(u,v)\leq\tilde{w}(u,v)\leq w(u,v)(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>∈</mo><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><mrow><mi>w</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mi>w</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)\in E,w(u,v)\leq\tilde{w}(u,v)\leq w(u,v)(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math>.</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx1.p6" class="ltx_para ltx_noindent">
<p id="A2.SSx1.p6.1" class="ltx_p">In other words, the approximate weights maintained for each edge are an upper-bound
on the true edge weight. Note that we can compute the <span id="A2.SSx1.p6.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">exact weight</span> of any edge in
<math id="A2.SSx1.p6.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(1)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(1)</annotation></semantics></math> at any given time since the cut weight, and cluster sizes are exact; we need the approximate weights and <a href="#Thminvariant1" title="Invariant 1. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">1</span></a>
only when computing <math id="A2.SSx1.p6.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="w_{\max}(u)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">w_{\max}(u)</annotation></semantics></math>, since we cannot
afford to scan over all incident edges to <math id="A2.SSx1.p6.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math> to compute <math id="A2.SSx1.p6.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="w_{\max}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">w_{\max}</annotation></semantics></math> exactly.</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx1.p7" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx1.p7.1" class="ltx_p">Let <math id="A2.SSx1.p7.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math> be the <span id="A2.SSx1.p7.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">stored goodness</span> of the edge <math id="A2.SSx1.p7.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math>, i.e. the
stored goodness value in the priority queue <math id="A2.SSx1.p7.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="A(u)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A(u)</annotation></semantics></math> if <math id="A2.SSx1.p7.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math> is assigned to <math id="A2.SSx1.p7.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math>.
At initialization, <math id="A2.SSx1.p7.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}=\tilde{g}=g" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}=\tilde{g}=g</annotation></semantics></math>. However, as merges start
to occur in the graph, the stored goodness values, <math id="A2.SSx1.p7.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}</annotation></semantics></math> may become approximate. Understanding
the stored goodness values is necessary, since when scanning the assigned edges of a vertex to find a potentially
mergeable edge, we use the stored goodness values, <math id="A2.SSx1.p7.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}</annotation></semantics></math>. The next invariant summarizes the relationship between
the <math id="A2.SSx1.p7.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}</annotation></semantics></math> values and the exact goodness at any point in time in the algorithm (we provide the proof that the
invariant is maintained in Lemma <a href="#A2.Thmtheorem3" title="Lemma 0. ‣ Correctness ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">3</span></a>).</p>
</div>
<div id="Thminvariant2" class="ltx_theorem ltx_theorem_invariant">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_smallcaps ltx_title_theorem">Invariant 2.</h6>
<div id="Thminvariant2.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="Thminvariant2.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="Thminvariant2.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">For every edge <math id="Thminvariant2.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,v)\in E" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>∈</mo><mi>E</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)\in E</annotation></semantics></math>, <math id="Thminvariant2.p1.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq g(u,v)(1+\alpha)^{2}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><msup><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq g(u,v)(1+\alpha)^{2}</annotation></semantics></math></span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx1.p8" class="ltx_para ltx_noindent">
<p id="A2.SSx1.p8.1" class="ltx_p">So at any point in time during the algorithm, if the goodness value of an edge
is small, then the stored goodness value of the edge is also small. Again, we emphasize the difference
between the three types of goodness values: <math id="A2.SSx1.p8.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>g</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g</annotation></semantics></math> is the true goodness value of an edge;
<math id="A2.SSx1.p8.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{g}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{g}</annotation></semantics></math> is a <math id="A2.SSx1.p8.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> over-approximation of the <math id="A2.SSx1.p8.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>g</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g</annotation></semantics></math> value that we can compute
at any instant; finally, <math id="A2.SSx1.p8.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}</annotation></semantics></math> is the goodness value that is stored in the priority queue of
the assigned endpoint.
We further note that the invariant says nothing about a lower bound on <math id="A2.SSx1.p8.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math> in terms of <math id="A2.SSx1.p8.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math>.
For example, it could be
the case that <math id="A2.SSx1.p8.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g(u,v)\gg(1+\epsilon)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≫</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g(u,v)\gg(1+\epsilon)</annotation></semantics></math> while <math id="A2.SSx1.p8.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq(1+\epsilon)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq(1+\epsilon)</annotation></semantics></math>,
but this is not an issue, as we can amortize the cost of checking the edge against the
increase in goodness for the edge.</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx1.p9" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx1.p9.1" class="ltx_p">Initially,
<a href="#Thminvariant1" title="Invariant 1. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">1</span></a> is satisfied since the weights are exact.
Lastly, <a href="#Thminvariant2" title="Invariant 2. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">2</span></a> is satisfied initially since the goodness values are exact.</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx1.p10" class="ltx_para ltx_noindent">
<p id="A2.SSx1.p10.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.SSx1.p10.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_bold">Useful Lemmas.</span>
Next, we state some useful lemmas about the approximation error
of computing <math id="A2.SSx1.p10.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}_{\max}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}_{\max}</annotation></semantics></math> and <math id="A2.SSx1.p10.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{g}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{g}</annotation></semantics></math> at a given point in time,
based on the invariants above.</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem1" class="ltx_theorem ltx_theorem_lemma">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_smallcaps ltx_title_theorem">Lemma 0.</h6>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem1.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.Thmtheorem1.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><math id="A2.Thmtheorem1.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\forall u\in V" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mo rspace="0.167em">∀</mo><mi>u</mi></mrow><mo>∈</mo><mi>V</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\forall u\in V</annotation></semantics></math><span id="A2.Thmtheorem1.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">, <math id="A2.Thmtheorem1.p1.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="w_{\max}(u)\leq\tilde{w}_{\max}(u)\leq w_{\max}(u)(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">w_{\max}(u)\leq\tilde{w}_{\max}(u)\leq w_{\max}(u)(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math>.</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx1.p11" class="ltx_para ltx_noindent">
<p id="A2.SSx1.p11.1" class="ltx_p">In other words, the approximate <math id="A2.SSx1.p11.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}_{\max}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}_{\max}</annotation></semantics></math> values s a <math id="A2.SSx1.p11.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> over-approximation of the true <math id="A2.SSx1.p11.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="w_{\max}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">w_{\max}</annotation></semantics></math> values.</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx1.1" class="ltx_proof">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_italic ltx_title_proof">Proof.</h6>
<div id="A2.SSx1.p12" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx1.p12.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.SSx1.p12.1.1" class="ltx_text">Since we store a map indexed on the approximate partial weights of the neighbors and sorted
by the approximate edge weights <math id="A2.SSx1.p12.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}</annotation></semantics></math>, we can simply use the largest <math id="A2.SSx1.p12.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}</annotation></semantics></math> of an
incident edge to be <math id="A2.SSx1.p12.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}_{\max}(u)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}_{\max}(u)</annotation></semantics></math>.
The returned weight is a <math id="A2.SSx1.p12.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> approximation to <math id="A2.SSx1.p12.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="w_{\max}{}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">w_{\max}{}</annotation></semantics></math> by Invariant <a href="#Thminvariant1" title="Invariant 1. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">1</span></a>.
∎</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx1.p13" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx1.p13.1" class="ltx_p">Using <a href="#A2.Thmtheorem1" title="Lemma 0. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">Lemma</span> <span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">1</span></a>, we obtain guarantees on calculating a
particular edge’s goodness value. For the calculation, we use the
exact value of the edge weight, but suffer an approximation in the numerator;
when computing <math id="A2.SSx1.p13.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\max(\tilde{w}_{\max}(u),\tilde{w}_{\max}(v))" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>max</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\max(\tilde{w}_{\max}(u),\tilde{w}_{\max}(v))</annotation></semantics></math>, the value can be at most
a multiplicative <math id="A2.SSx1.p13.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor larger than the true value. This
is summarized in the next lemma:</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem2" class="ltx_theorem ltx_theorem_lemma">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_smallcaps ltx_title_theorem">Lemma 0.</h6>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem2.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.Thmtheorem2.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><math id="A2.Thmtheorem2.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\forall(u,v)\in E" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mo>∀</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>∈</mo><mi>E</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\forall(u,v)\in E</annotation></semantics></math><span id="A2.Thmtheorem2.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">, <math id="A2.Thmtheorem2.p1.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g(u,v)\leq\tilde{g}(u,v)\leq g(u,v)(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g(u,v)\leq\tilde{g}(u,v)\leq g(u,v)(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math>.</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx1.2" class="ltx_proof">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_italic ltx_title_proof">Proof.</h6>
<div id="A2.SSx1.p14" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx1.p14.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.SSx1.p14.1.1" class="ltx_text">The approximate goodness of an edge is <math id="A2.SSx1.p14.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="U/L" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>U</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>L</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">U/L</annotation></semantics></math> where
<math id="A2.SSx1.p14.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="U=\max(\tilde{w}_{\max}(u),\tilde{w}_{\max}(v))" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mi>max</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">U=\max(\tilde{w}_{\max}(u),\tilde{w}_{\max}(v))</annotation></semantics></math> and <math id="A2.SSx1.p14.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="L=\min(\mathrm{M}(u),\mathrm{M}(v),w(u,v))" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>L</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mi>min</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><mi>w</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">L=\min(\mathrm{M}(u),\mathrm{M}(v),w(u,v))</annotation></semantics></math>.
Since <math id="A2.SSx1.p14.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="U" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>U</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">U</annotation></semantics></math> is a <math id="A2.SSx1.p14.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> approximation of the true numerator and cannot
be smaller, and <math id="A2.SSx1.p14.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="L" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>L</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">L</annotation></semantics></math> is exact, the worst case is when <math id="A2.SSx1.p14.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="U" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>U</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">U</annotation></semantics></math> is a <math id="A2.SSx1.p14.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> approximation.
Therefore, we have <math id="A2.SSx1.p14.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g(u,v)\leq\tilde{g}(u,v)\leq(1+\alpha)g(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mi>g</mi><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g(u,v)\leq\tilde{g}(u,v)\leq(1+\alpha)g(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math>.
∎</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx1.p15" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx1.p15.1" class="ltx_p">We describe how <math id="A2.SSx1.p15.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}</annotation></semantics></math> and <math id="A2.SSx1.p15.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{g}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{g}</annotation></semantics></math> are calculated,
and why the lemmas are true in more detail below.
<a href="#A2.Thmtheorem2" title="Lemma 0. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">Lemma</span> <span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">2</span></a> is crucial since by only merging edges where
<math id="A2.SSx1.p15.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{g}(u,v)\leq(1+\epsilon)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{g}(u,v)\leq(1+\epsilon)</annotation></semantics></math>, the edge must truly be <math id="A2.SSx1.p15.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\epsilon)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\epsilon)</annotation></semantics></math>-good.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section id="A2.SSx2" class="ltx_subsection">
<h3 class="ltx_title ltx_title_subsection">Graph Representation Operations</h3>

<div id="A2.SSx2.p1" class="ltx_para ltx_noindent">
<p id="A2.SSx2.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.SSx2.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_bold">Merge<math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math>.</span>
We merge the vertex with smaller cluster size into the vertex with larger
cluster size. Suppose we are
merging <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math> into <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math> (thus <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math> will still be active after the merge).
Both <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math> and <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math> are initially active.
Merging the weights can be done in <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(|N(u)|)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">|</mo><mrow><mi>N</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">|</mo></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(|N(u)|)</annotation></semantics></math> time by iterating over
edges in <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="N(u)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>N</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">N(u)</annotation></semantics></math> and updating weights in <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="N(v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>N</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">N(v)</annotation></semantics></math> using the average-linkage
formula.
After merging the neighborhoods, we push all edges in <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m10" class="ltx_Math" alttext="A(u)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A(u)</annotation></semantics></math>
(edges assigned to <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m11" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math>) to <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m12" class="ltx_Math" alttext="R" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>R</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">R</annotation></semantics></math> to be fixed at the end of the merge.
Finally, we see if we need to perform a <span id="A2.SSx2.p1.1.2" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">broadcast</span> operation due to
either the cluster size increasing sufficiently, or the <math id="A2.SSx2.p1.m13" class="ltx_Math" alttext="w_{\max}{}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">w_{\max}{}</annotation></semantics></math> value
decreasing sufficiently:</p>
<ol id="A2.I1" class="ltx_enumerate">
<li id="A2.I1.i1" class="ltx_item" style="list-style-type:none;"><span class="ltx_tag ltx_tag_item">(1)</span> 
<div id="A2.I1.i1.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.I1.i1.p1.1" class="ltx_p">If <math id="A2.I1.i1.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math>’s cluster size grows larger by a <math id="A2.I1.i1.p1.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor (from the last broadcast value), we iterate over all edges in <math id="A2.I1.i1.p1.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="N(v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>N</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">N(v)</annotation></semantics></math> and update their weights in both <math id="A2.I1.i1.p1.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math> and <math id="A2.I1.i1.p1.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math>’s neighbor’s queues.</p>
</div></li>
<li id="A2.I1.i2" class="ltx_item" style="list-style-type:none;"><span class="ltx_tag ltx_tag_item">(2)</span> 
<div id="A2.I1.i2.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.I1.i2.p1.1" class="ltx_p">If <math id="A2.I1.i2.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}_{\max}(v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}_{\max}(v)</annotation></semantics></math> either grows or decreases by a <math id="A2.I1.i2.p1.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor from the last value of <math id="A2.I1.i2.p1.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}_{\max}(v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}_{\max}(v)</annotation></semantics></math> that a broadcast occurred at, we iterate over all of <math id="A2.I1.i2.p1.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math>’s edges and reassign them to the endpoint with larger <math id="A2.I1.i2.p1.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}_{\max}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}_{\max}</annotation></semantics></math> value.</p>
</div></li>
</ol>
<p id="A2.SSx2.p1.2" class="ltx_p">This concludes the description of the merge procedure.
We describe the correctness details (i.e., showing that the invariants
are preserved after this operation) shortly.</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx2.p2" class="ltx_para ltx_noindent">
<p id="A2.SSx2.p2.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.SSx2.p2.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_bold">BestAssignedNeighbor<math id="A2.SSx2.p2.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u)</annotation></semantics></math>.</span>
This operation is used to compute the min goodness assigned neighbor of <math id="A2.SSx2.p2.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math>
in Algorithm <a href="#alg4" title="Algorithm 4 ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">4</span></a>.
Similar to <math id="A2.SSx2.p2.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="w_{\max}{}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">w_{\max}{}</annotation></semantics></math>, getting the <span id="A2.SSx2.p2.1.2" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">true</span> best assigned neighbor would
require scanning all edges assigned to <math id="A2.SSx2.p2.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math>.
Instead, we iterate over every edge in <math id="A2.SSx2.p2.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="A(u)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A(u)</annotation></semantics></math> in increasing order
of the <span id="A2.SSx2.p2.1.3" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">stored goodness value</span>, <math id="A2.SSx2.p2.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math>
until we either find an edge whose approximate goodness <math id="A2.SSx2.p2.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{g}(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{g}(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math>
is smaller than <math id="A2.SSx2.p2.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\epsilon)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\epsilon)</annotation></semantics></math>, or we exhaust all edges with
<math id="A2.SSx2.p2.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math> in the range <math id="A2.SSx2.p2.m10" class="ltx_Math" alttext="[1,(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)]" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[1,(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)]</annotation></semantics></math>.</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx2.p3" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx2.p3.1" class="ltx_p">Each edge unsuccessfully considered in this range has
<math id="A2.SSx2.p3.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math>, but
<math id="A2.SSx2.p3.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{g}(u,v)&gt;1+\epsilon" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>&gt;</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{g}(u,v)&gt;1+\epsilon</annotation></semantics></math>.
When analyzing the running time, this gap helps show
that an edge can be unsuccessfully checked only <math id="A2.SSx2.p3.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(\log n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log n)</annotation></semantics></math> times.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section id="A2.SSx3" class="ltx_subsection">
<h3 class="ltx_title ltx_title_subsection">Correctness</h3>

<div id="A2.Thmtheorem3" class="ltx_theorem ltx_theorem_lemma">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_smallcaps ltx_title_theorem">Lemma 0.</h6>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem3.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.Thmtheorem3.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.Thmtheorem3.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">The Merge<math id="A2.Thmtheorem3.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math> operation preserves <a href="#Thminvariant1" title="Invariant 1. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">1</span></a> and <a href="#Thminvariant2" title="Invariant 2. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">2</span></a>.</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx3.1" class="ltx_proof">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_italic ltx_title_proof">Proof.</h6>
<div id="A2.SSx3.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx3.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.SSx3.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text">Suppose that the invariants are true before the merge operation, and suppose
<math id="A2.SSx3.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math> merges into <math id="A2.SSx3.p1.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math> (i.e. <math id="A2.SSx3.p1.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math> remains active at the end of the merge).</span></p>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx3.p2" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx3.p2.1" class="ltx_p"><a href="#Thminvariant1" title="Invariant 1. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref ltx_font_italic"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">1</span></a><span id="A2.SSx3.p2.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">.</span><span id="A2.SSx3.p2.1.2" class="ltx_text"> The only edges whose weights are affected
by the merge are those incident to <math id="A2.SSx3.p2.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math>. Let <math id="A2.SSx3.p2.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(v,x)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(v,x)</annotation></semantics></math> be such an edge.
If <math id="A2.SSx3.p2.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,x)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,x)</annotation></semantics></math> was in <math id="A2.SSx3.p2.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="G" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>G</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">G</annotation></semantics></math> previously, then the weight of the <math id="A2.SSx3.p2.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(v,x)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(v,x)</annotation></semantics></math> edge will
be exact. Otherwise, <a href="#Thminvariant1" title="Invariant 1. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">1</span></a> previously held for <math id="A2.SSx3.p2.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(v,x)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(v,x)</annotation></semantics></math>. It is clear
that <a href="#Thminvariant1" title="Invariant 1. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">1</span></a> continues to hold if the cluster size of <math id="A2.SSx3.p2.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math> has not yet
grown by a factor of <math id="A2.SSx3.p2.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math>; on the other hand if it grows by more than this
factor, <math id="A2.SSx3.p2.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math> broadcasts, and the <math id="A2.SSx3.p2.m10" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(v,x)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(v,x)</annotation></semantics></math> weight will be exact.</span></p>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx3.p3" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx3.p3.1" class="ltx_p"><a href="#Thminvariant2" title="Invariant 2. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref ltx_font_italic"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">2</span></a><span id="A2.SSx3.p3.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">.</span><span id="A2.SSx3.p3.1.2" class="ltx_text"> Consider an arbitrary <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(x,y)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(x,y)</annotation></semantics></math> edge. When the edge was last assigned, say to <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="x" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>x</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation></semantics></math>,
<math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(x,y)=\tilde{g}(x,y)\leq(1+\alpha)g(x,y)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mi>g</mi><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(x,y)=\tilde{g}(x,y)\leq(1+\alpha)g(x,y)</annotation></semantics></math>.
The invariant can be violated if
<math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g(x,y)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g(x,y)</annotation></semantics></math> decreases, which can only occur by
<math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\max(w_{\max}(x),w_{\max}(y))" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>max</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\max(w_{\max}(x),w_{\max}(y))</annotation></semantics></math> decreasing.
The algorithm will update the goodness of <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(x,y)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(x,y)</annotation></semantics></math> once <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\max(\tilde{w}_{\max}(x),\tilde{w}_{\max}(y))" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>max</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\max(\tilde{w}_{\max}(x),\tilde{w}_{\max}(y))</annotation></semantics></math>
decreases by a <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor.
Since <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\max(\tilde{w}_{\max}(x),\tilde{w}_{\max}(y))\leq(1+\alpha)\max(w_{\max}(x),w_{\max}(y))" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>max</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo lspace="0.167em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mi>max</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\max(\tilde{w}_{\max}(x),\tilde{w}_{\max}(y))\leq(1+\alpha)\max(w_{\max}(x),w_{\max}(y))</annotation></semantics></math>,
after <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m10" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\max(w_{\max}(x),w_{\max}(y))" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>max</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><msub><mi>w</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\max(w_{\max}(x),w_{\max}(y))</annotation></semantics></math> decreases by a <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m11" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor,
<math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m12" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\max(\tilde{w}_{\max}(x),\tilde{w}_{\max}(y))" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>max</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\max(\tilde{w}_{\max}(x),\tilde{w}_{\max}(y))</annotation></semantics></math> must also decrease by a <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m13" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor.
At this point, we perform a broadcast and update the goodness of this edge to
<math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m14" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{g}(x,y)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{g}(x,y)</annotation></semantics></math>. The ratio of <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m15" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(x,y)/g(x,y)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>/</mo><mi>g</mi></mrow><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(x,y)/g(x,y)</annotation></semantics></math> is
always upper bounded by <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m16" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)^{2}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msup><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)^{2}</annotation></semantics></math>, and is largest
when initially <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m17" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g(x,y)=\ddot{g}(x,y)/(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g(x,y)=\ddot{g}(x,y)/(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> and <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m18" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math>
decreases by a <math id="A2.SSx3.p3.m19" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor.
∎</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem4" class="ltx_theorem ltx_theorem_lemma">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_smallcaps ltx_title_theorem">Lemma 0.</h6>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem4.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.Thmtheorem4.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.Thmtheorem4.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">Once the algorithm terminates, no edges with goodness (<math id="A2.Thmtheorem4.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>g</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g</annotation></semantics></math>)
in the range <math id="A2.Thmtheorem4.p1.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="[1,T]" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>T</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[1,T]</annotation></semantics></math> with <math id="A2.Thmtheorem4.p1.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="T=(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)^{3}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>/</mo><msup><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">T=(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)^{3}</annotation></semantics></math> remain.</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx3.2" class="ltx_proof">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_italic ltx_title_proof">Proof.</h6>
<div id="A2.SSx3.p4" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx3.p4.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.SSx3.p4.1.1" class="ltx_text">Suppose otherwise. Let this edge be <math id="A2.SSx3.p4.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math> and its goodness value be <math id="A2.SSx3.p4.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g_{uv}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mi>g</mi><mrow><mi>u</mi><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mi>v</mi></mrow></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g_{uv}</annotation></semantics></math>.
Suppose <math id="A2.SSx3.p4.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math> is assigned to <math id="A2.SSx3.p4.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math>.
By <a href="#Thminvariant2" title="Invariant 2. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">2</span></a>, <math id="A2.SSx3.p4.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq g(u,v)(1+\alpha)^{2}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><msup><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq g(u,v)(1+\alpha)^{2}</annotation></semantics></math>.
Since <math id="A2.SSx3.p4.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="g_{uv}\leq T=(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)^{3}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>g</mi><mrow><mi>u</mi><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mi>v</mi></mrow></msub><mo>≤</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>/</mo><msup><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">g_{uv}\leq T=(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)^{3}</annotation></semantics></math>, by
<a href="#Thminvariant2" title="Invariant 2. ‣ Graph Representation ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">2</span></a>, <math id="A2.SSx3.p4.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq g_{uv}(1+\alpha)\leq(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><msub><mi>g</mi><mrow><mi>u</mi><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mi>v</mi></mrow></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq g_{uv}(1+\alpha)\leq(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math>.
However, this means that the algorithm incorrectly missed an
edge in the range <math id="A2.SSx3.p4.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="[0,(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)]" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[0,(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)]</annotation></semantics></math> for node <math id="A2.SSx3.p4.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math>,
contradicting the definition of BestAssignedNeighbor<math id="A2.SSx3.p4.m10" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u)</annotation></semantics></math>.
∎</span></p>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<section id="A2.SSx4" class="ltx_subsection">
<h3 class="ltx_title ltx_title_subsection">Running Time Analysis</h3>

<div id="A2.SSx4.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx4.p1.1" class="ltx_p">We will show that <math id="A2.SSx4.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(\log n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log n)</annotation></semantics></math> total broadcasts and reassignment operations
are performed for any vertex over the course of the algorithm.</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx4.p2" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx4.p2.1" class="ltx_p">Let <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="a_{1},\ldots,a_{k}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">…</mi><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>k</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a_{1},\ldots,a_{k}</annotation></semantics></math> be the sizes of the cluster containing
a vertex <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="a" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>a</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a</annotation></semantics></math>. We have <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="a_{1}=1" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a_{1}=1</annotation></semantics></math> and <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="a_{k}\leq n" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>k</mi></msub><mo>≤</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a_{k}\leq n</annotation></semantics></math>.
When a cluster of size <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="p" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>p</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">p</annotation></semantics></math> merges with a cluster of size <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="q" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>q</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">q</annotation></semantics></math> (<math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="p\geq q" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>≥</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">p\geq q</annotation></semantics></math>),
the <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}_{\max}(p)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>p</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}_{\max}(p)</annotation></semantics></math> value can increase from <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="w" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>w</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">w</annotation></semantics></math> to at most <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m10" class="ltx_Math" alttext="w((1+\epsilon)q+p)/(p+q)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>w</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">w((1+\epsilon)q+p)/(p+q)</annotation></semantics></math>,
which is a multiplicative increase of <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m11" class="ltx_Math" alttext="((1+\epsilon)q+p)/(p+q)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">((1+\epsilon)q+p)/(p+q)</annotation></semantics></math>.
The increase is maximized when <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m12" class="ltx_Math" alttext="p=q" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">p=q</annotation></semantics></math>, for an
increase of at most <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m13" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(2+\epsilon)/2" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(2+\epsilon)/2</annotation></semantics></math>.
We would like to show that the overall increase in <math id="A2.SSx4.p2.m14" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}_{\max}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}_{\max}</annotation></semantics></math> over
any sequence of merges cannot be too large.
We will use the following lemma for the analysis:</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem5" class="ltx_theorem ltx_theorem_lemma">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_smallcaps ltx_title_theorem">Lemma 0.</h6>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem5.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.Thmtheorem5.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.Thmtheorem5.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">Let <math id="A2.Thmtheorem5.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="a_{1}&lt;a_{2}&lt;\ldots&lt;a_{k}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&lt;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">…</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>k</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a_{1}&lt;a_{2}&lt;\ldots&lt;a_{k}</annotation></semantics></math>.
Then, <math id="A2.Thmtheorem5.p1.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\prod_{i=1}^{k-1}(1+\frac{t(a_{i+1}-a_{i})}{a_{i+1}})=O(a_{k}^{t})" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><msubsup><mo>∏</mo><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mo lspace="0em" stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>−</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>i</mi></msub></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mfrac></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mi>k</mi><mi>t</mi></msubsup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\prod_{i=1}^{k-1}(1+\frac{t(a_{i+1}-a_{i})}{a_{i+1}})=O(a_{k}^{t})</annotation></semantics></math>.</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx4.1" class="ltx_proof">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_italic ltx_title_proof">Proof.</h6>
<div id="A2.SSx4.p3" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx4.p3.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.SSx4.p3.1.1" class="ltx_text">Since <math id="A2.SSx4.p3.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="1+x\leq e^{x}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>≤</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mi>x</mi></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1+x\leq e^{x}</annotation></semantics></math>, for any <math id="A2.SSx4.p3.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="x_{1},\ldots,x_{k}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>x</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">…</mi><mo>,</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mi>k</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x_{1},\ldots,x_{k}</annotation></semantics></math> we have</span></p>
<table id="A2.Ex8" class="ltx_equation ltx_eqn_table">

<tbody><tr class="ltx_equation ltx_eqn_row ltx_align_baseline">
<td class="ltx_eqn_cell ltx_eqn_center_padleft"></td>
<td class="ltx_eqn_cell ltx_align_center"><math id="A2.Ex8.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\prod_{i=1}^{k}(1+x_{i})\leq e^{\sum_{i=1}^{k}x_{i}}." display="block" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mrow><munderover><mo movablelimits="false">∏</mo><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>k</mi></munderover><mrow><mo lspace="0em" stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>x</mi><mi>i</mi></msub></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mstyle displaystyle="false"><msubsup><mo maxsize="0.700em" minsize="0.700em" stretchy="true">∑</mo><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mi>k</mi></msubsup></mstyle><msub><mi>x</mi><mi>i</mi></msub></mrow></msup></mrow><mo lspace="0em">.</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\prod_{i=1}^{k}(1+x_{i})\leq e^{\sum_{i=1}^{k}x_{i}}.</annotation></semantics></math></td>

</table>

          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">B</span></a>. Observe that</span></p>
