</div>
<div id="A2.SSx4.p4" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx4.p4.1" class="ltx_p">By setting <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="t=\epsilon" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">t=\epsilon</annotation></semantics></math>, and <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="a_{i+1}=p+q" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a_{i+1}=p+q</annotation></semantics></math> and <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="a_{i}=p" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>i</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a_{i}=p</annotation></semantics></math>,
<math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="1+\frac{t(a_{i+1}-a_{i})}{a_{i+1}}=((1+\epsilon)q+p)/(p+q)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>−</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>i</mi></msub></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mfrac></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1+\frac{t(a_{i+1}-a_{i})}{a_{i+1}}=((1+\epsilon)q+p)/(p+q)</annotation></semantics></math>.
Therefore, if <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="a_{1}&lt;a_{2}&lt;\ldots&lt;a_{k}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>&lt;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">…</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>k</mi></msub></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a_{1}&lt;a_{2}&lt;\ldots&lt;a_{k}</annotation></semantics></math> are the cluster sizes of a vertex <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="v" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>v</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation></semantics></math>,
then, <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\prod_{i=1}^{k-1}(1+\frac{\epsilon(a_{i+1}-a_{i})}{a_{i+1}})=O(a_{k}^{\epsilon})=O(n^{\epsilon})" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><msubsup><mo>∏</mo><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mo lspace="0em" stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>−</mo><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>i</mi></msub></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><msub><mi>a</mi><mrow><mi>i</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mfrac></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mi>k</mi><mi>ϵ</mi></msubsup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mi>ϵ</mi></msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\prod_{i=1}^{k-1}(1+\frac{\epsilon(a_{i+1}-a_{i})}{a_{i+1}})=O(a_{k}^{\epsilon})=O(n^{\epsilon})</annotation></semantics></math>.
Therefore, the total increase in <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{w}_{\max}(v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{w}_{\max}(v)</annotation></semantics></math> over the
course of the algorithm is at most <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(n^{\epsilon})" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mi>ϵ</mi></msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(n^{\epsilon})</annotation></semantics></math>
Assuming a polynomially bounded aspect ratio, i.e., letting
<math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m10" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\mathcal{W}_{\max}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mi class="ltx_font_mathcaligraphic">𝒲</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mathcal{W}_{\max}</annotation></semantics></math> be the largest edge weight initially
and <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m11" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\mathcal{W}_{\min}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mi class="ltx_font_mathcaligraphic">𝒲</mi><mi>min</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mathcal{W}_{\min}</annotation></semantics></math> be the smallest edge weight initially,
we have that <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m12" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\mathcal{W}_{\max}/\mathcal{W}_{\min}\in O(\mathsf{poly}(n))" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><msub><mi class="ltx_font_mathcaligraphic">𝒲</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo>/</mo><msub><mi class="ltx_font_mathcaligraphic">𝒲</mi><mi>min</mi></msub></mrow><mo>∈</mo><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>𝗉𝗈𝗅𝗒</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>n</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mathcal{W}_{\max}/\mathcal{W}_{\min}\in O(\mathsf{poly}(n))</annotation></semantics></math>.
Therefore the overall range for the best values of a
vertex over a sequence of merges is also <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m13" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(\mathsf{poly}(n))" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>𝗉𝗈𝗅𝗒</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>n</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\mathsf{poly}(n))</annotation></semantics></math>.
For any constant <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m14" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\alpha&gt;0" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha&gt;0</annotation></semantics></math>, the total number of broadcasts
performed by the vertex over its lifetime is <math id="A2.SSx4.p4.m15" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(\log n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log n)</annotation></semantics></math>. This is summarized
in the following lemma:</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem6" class="ltx_theorem ltx_theorem_lemma">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_smallcaps ltx_title_theorem">Lemma 0.</h6>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem6.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.Thmtheorem6.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.Thmtheorem6.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">For any constants <math id="A2.Thmtheorem6.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\epsilon&gt;0,\alpha&gt;0" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\epsilon&gt;0,\alpha&gt;0</annotation></semantics></math>, and a graph with polynomially
bounded aspect ratio, the total number of broadcast operations performed by
any vertex over the sequence of the algorithm is <math id="A2.Thmtheorem6.p1.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(\log n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log n)</annotation></semantics></math>.</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx4.p5" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx4.p5.1" class="ltx_p">Next, we show that the total number of times a given edge <math id="A2.SSx4.p5.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math>
in unsuccessfully checked is low. We use the same constants and setting
(bounded aspect ratio):</p>
</div>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem7" class="ltx_theorem ltx_theorem_lemma">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_smallcaps ltx_title_theorem">Lemma 0.</h6>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem7.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.Thmtheorem7.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.Thmtheorem7.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">Each edge <math id="A2.Thmtheorem7.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math> is checked
unsuccessfully over all calls to BestAssignedNeighbor<math id="A2.Thmtheorem7.p1.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u)</annotation></semantics></math>
at most <math id="A2.Thmtheorem7.p1.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(\log n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log n)</annotation></semantics></math> times.</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx4.2" class="ltx_proof">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_italic ltx_title_proof">Proof.</h6>
<div id="A2.SSx4.p6" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx4.p6.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.SSx4.p6.1.1" class="ltx_text">Recall that a check of an edge <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math> is unsuccessful if <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(u,v)\leq(1+\epsilon)/(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math>
but <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{g}(u,v)&gt;(1+\epsilon)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>&gt;</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{g}(u,v)&gt;(1+\epsilon)</annotation></semantics></math>.
Since <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\ddot{g}(u,v)=\tilde{g}(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>¨</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ddot{g}(u,v)=\tilde{g}(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math> when this edge
was assigned to <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math>, in the meantime, <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\tilde{g}(u,v)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>g</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\tilde{g}(u,v)</annotation></semantics></math> grew
by at least a <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m7" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor.
This could occur by <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m8" class="ltx_Math" alttext="U=\tilde{w}_{\max}(u)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>U</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><msub><mover accent="true"><mi>w</mi><mo>~</mo></mover><mi>max</mi></msub><mo lspace="0em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">U=\tilde{w}_{\max}(u)</annotation></semantics></math> growing
by a <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m9" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor, or <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m10" class="ltx_Math" alttext="D=\min(\mathrm{M}(u),\mathrm{M}(v),w(u,v))" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mi>min</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mrow><mi>w</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>v</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D=\min(\mathrm{M}(u),\mathrm{M}(v),w(u,v))</annotation></semantics></math>
decreasing by a <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m11" class="ltx_Math" alttext="(1+\alpha)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(1+\alpha)</annotation></semantics></math> factor, or some combination of the two.
In either case, either <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m12" class="ltx_Math" alttext="U" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>U</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">U</annotation></semantics></math> must grow or <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m13" class="ltx_Math" alttext="D" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>D</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D</annotation></semantics></math> must shrink by at least a <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m14" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\sqrt{1+\alpha}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msqrt><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></msqrt><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sqrt{1+\alpha}</annotation></semantics></math> factor i.e., a constant <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m15" class="ltx_Math" alttext="&gt;1" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mphantom></mphantom><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">&gt;1</annotation></semantics></math>.
Using the same idea as the proof of Lemma <a href="#A2.Thmtheorem6" title="Lemma 0. ‣ Running Time Analysis ‣ Appendix B 
        
          SubgraphHAC ‣ TeraHAC: Hierarchical Agglomerative Clustering of Trillion-Edge Graphs" class="ltx_ref"><span class="ltx_text ltx_ref_tag">6</span></a>,
since <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m16" class="ltx_Math" alttext="G" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>G</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">G</annotation></semantics></math> has bounded aspect ratio, the total amount <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m17" class="ltx_Math" alttext="U" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>U</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">U</annotation></semantics></math> can grow is <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m18" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\log_{1+\alpha}n^{1+\epsilon}=O(\log n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mrow><msub><mi>log</mi><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></msub><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\log_{1+\alpha}n^{1+\epsilon}=O(\log n)</annotation></semantics></math>, and so the number of unsuccessful checks to due <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m19" class="ltx_Math" alttext="U" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>U</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">U</annotation></semantics></math> is <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m20" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(\log n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log n)</annotation></semantics></math>.
Similarly, since the three terms in <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m21" class="ltx_Math" alttext="D" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>D</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D</annotation></semantics></math> can be at most <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m22" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\mathcal{W}_{\max}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><msub><mi class="ltx_font_mathcaligraphic">𝒲</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mathcal{W}_{\max}</annotation></semantics></math>,
and can become no smaller than <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m23" class="ltx_Math" alttext="\mathcal{W}_{\min}/n^{2}" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi class="ltx_font_mathcaligraphic">𝒲</mi><mi>min</mi></msub><mo>/</mo><msup><mi>n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mathcal{W}_{\min}/n^{2}</annotation></semantics></math>, the overall
number of unsuccessful checks due to <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m24" class="ltx_Math" alttext="D" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>D</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D</annotation></semantics></math> is also <math id="A2.SSx4.p6.m25" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(\log n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log n)</annotation></semantics></math>, completing the proof.
∎</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem8" class="ltx_theorem ltx_theorem_theorem">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_smallcaps ltx_title_theorem">Theorem 8.</h6>
<div id="A2.Thmtheorem8.p1" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.Thmtheorem8.p1.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.Thmtheorem8.p1.1.1" class="ltx_text ltx_font_italic">The overall running time of the algorithm is <math id="A2.Thmtheorem8.p1.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O((m+n)\log^{2}n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><mo lspace="0.167em" rspace="0em">​</mo><msup><mi>log</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo lspace="0.167em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O((m+n)\log^{2}n)</annotation></semantics></math>.</span></p>
</div>
</div>
<div id="A2.SSx4.3" class="ltx_proof">
<h6 class="ltx_title ltx_runin ltx_font_italic ltx_title_proof">Proof.</h6>
<div id="A2.SSx4.p7" class="ltx_para">
<p id="A2.SSx4.p7.1" class="ltx_p"><span id="A2.SSx4.p7.1.1" class="ltx_text">Each broadcast requires updating the values of all neighbors of a node
<math id="A2.SSx4.p7.m1" class="ltx_Math" alttext="u" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mi>u</mi><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">u</annotation></semantics></math> in a heap at a cost of <math id="A2.SSx4.p7.m2" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(\log n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log n)</annotation></semantics></math> per edge. Since each node requires
at most <math id="A2.SSx4.p7.m3" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(\log n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log n)</annotation></semantics></math> broadcasts in total, the overall time is <math id="A2.SSx4.p7.m4" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(m\log^{2}n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo lspace="0.167em" rspace="0em">​</mo><msup><mi>log</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo lspace="0.167em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(m\log^{2}n)</annotation></semantics></math>.
Similarly, the cost over all calls to BestAssignedNeighbor is <math id="A2.SSx4.p7.m5" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(m\log^{2}n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo lspace="0.167em" rspace="0em">​</mo><msup><mi>log</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo lspace="0.167em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(m\log^{2}n)</annotation></semantics></math>
since each unsuccessful edge must be reinserted into a heap.
Lastly, the extract operations over the min heap indexed on the nodes costs
<math id="A2.SSx4.p7.m6" class="ltx_Math" alttext="O(n\log n)" display="inline" intent=":literal"><semantics><mrow><mi>O</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo lspace="0.167em" rspace="0em">​</mo><mrow><mi>log</mi><mo lspace="0.167em">⁡</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow></mrow><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(n\log n)</annotation></semantics></math> in total. Although the queue may be updated more times than this due to
cases where all edges in a call to BestAssignedNeighbor are unsuccessful, but
this cost can be charged to one of the unsuccessful edges.
∎</span></p>
</div>
</div>
</section>
</section>
</article>

    <script>
    // Auxiliary function, building the preview feature when
    // an inline citation is clicked
    function clicked_cite(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      let cite = this.closest('.ltx_cite');
      let next = cite.nextSibling;
      if (next && next.nodeType == Node.ELEMENT_NODE && next.getAttribute('class') == "ar5iv-bibitem-preview") {
        next.remove();
        return; }
      // Before adding a preview modal,
      // cleanup older previews, in case they're still open
      document.querySelectorAll('span.ar5iv-bibitem-preview').forEach(function(node) {
        node.remove();
      })

      // Create the preview
      preview = document.createElement('span');
      preview.setAttribute('class','ar5iv-bibitem-preview');
      let target = document.getElementById(this.getAttribute('href').slice(1));
      target.childNodes.forEach(function (child) {
        preview.append(child.cloneNode(true));
      });
      let close_x = document.createElement('button');
      close_x.setAttribute("aria-label","Close modal for bibliography item preview");
      close_x.textContent = "×";
      close_x.setAttribute('class', 'ar5iv-button-close-preview');
      close_x.setAttribute('onclick','this.parentNode.remove()');
      preview.append(close_x);
      preview.querySelectorAll('.ltx_tag_bibitem').forEach(function(node) {
        node.remove();
      });
      cite.parentNode.insertBefore(preview, cite.nextSibling);
      return;
    }
    // Global Document initialization:
    // - assign the preview feature to all inline citation links
    document.querySelectorAll(".ltx_cite .ltx_ref").forEach(function (link) {
      link.addEventListener("click", clicked_cite);
    });
    </script>
